**B. Active-set subgradient / Frank-Wolfe**
Compute convolution b. Define a surrogate smooth objective `phi(a)=sum over active lag neighborhoods of (b_k - M + eps)^2` or softmax. Minimize via projected gradient on simplex. Our coordinate scaling using exact can polish.

Implement a fast surrogate objective if metric unknown: use `surrogate_full` with central included if best_score>=2, else maybe weighted. We can attempt both. Direction: gradient of `sum_{k in active} w_k b_k`, where active full lags choose `b` values above `M*(1-alpha)`, maybe exclude central if offdiag? We can compute for both and take candidate directions:
- active offdiag only: active indices excluding central.
- active full: all.
- weighted toward uniform: direction e = 1/n - a (increase low, decrease high).
For gradient of b_m w.r.t a_i in full convolution? In full representation `b_m = ∑_j a_j a_{m-j}`. ∂/∂a_i = a_{m-i} + a_{i-m} if valid (two terms). Equivalent to autocorr lag. For nonnegative. We can compute efficiently by loops over active m:
```
grad = zeros(n)
for m in active:
    lo=max(0,m-n+1); hi=min(n-1,m)
    js=np.arange(lo,hi+1)
    # derivative: a[m-j] for each j plus a[j]? Actually derivative of b_m: sum_j [δ_{j=i} a_{m-i} + a_j δ_{m-j=i}?]. For each pair (j, m-j). The two contributions correspond to a_{m-j} and a_j? For each j in range, changing a_i: if j=i, add a_{m-i}; if m-j=i (j=m-i), add a_{m-j}. In vectorized over js of possible j, the derivative for all i can be done by for each j, add a[m-j] to index j; and add a_j to index m-j. But since m-j symmetric, this is 2*a? Let's derive: b_m = ∑_{j=lo}^{hi} a_j a_{m-j}. If we take derivative w.r.t a_i, terms where j=i: a_{m-i}, and where m-j=i => j=m-i: a_{m-i}, if m-j is in j-range and m-j != i; same if i = m/2 term derivative 2a_i? But in autocorr positive lag c_k, derivative is a_{i-k}+a_{i+k}. Full b_m for central m=n-1: derivative 2a_i (since each square) except? yes. For off m not central, derivative 2a_{i-k} where? Need formula.
Simpler: For full b_m, define lag l = m-(n-1). b_m = c_l = ∑_i a_i a_{i+l} where i valid (if l positive) or negative. Derivative: for any k with lag l, gradient +a_{i+l} if valid + a_{i-l} if valid. That is along two shifts. We can compute by shifts:
if l>=0:
  i where i+l<n: grad[i] += a[i+l]
  i where i-l>=0: grad[i] += a[i-l]
if l<0 similar. For central l=0: grad=2*a.
This works for full b array m = n-1+l.
For objective max over selected lags, subgradient = combine gradients with weights.
Then project onto simplex: a - η grad, clip, normalize. Use line search exact.
```
This can handle full or offdiag by choosing lags. We can run for both variants and exact-accept.

Implement F(x) vectorized:
```
def make_coord_func(i, a, b, C):
    y=a[i]; c0=1-y
    ks=np.arange(i, min(2*n, i+n))  # up to 2n-2
    js=ks-i
    is_quad = (js==i)
    old = np.where(is_quad, y*y, 2*y*a[js])
    coeff = np.where(is_quad, 0.0, 2*a[js])
    base = b[ks] - old
    def fx(x):
        vals = base + coeff*x
        if any quad:
           idx=quad_idx
           vals[idx] = base[idx] + x*x  # base for quad = b - y^2
        m = max(C, vals.max())
        return m/(c0+x)**2
```

```
def coordinate_descent(a, max_passes=20, time_limit=None, verbose=False):
    a = normalize(a)
    n=len(a); b=np.convolve(a,a)
    cur = 2*n*b.max()
    # cur score = 2n*M (since sum=1)
    best_a = a.copy(); best_cur=cur
    for pass in range(max_passes):
       improved=False; pass_improve=0
       order = np.random.permutation(n) if random else np.arange(n)
       for i in order:
          if time left < 0.5 break
          y=a[i]
          # if y==0? still optimize maybe increase
          ks=np.arange(i, min(2*n, i+n))
          js=ks-i
          quad = (js==i)
          coeff = np.where(quad, 0.0, 2.0*a[js])
          old = np.where(quad, y*y, 2.0*y*a[js])
          base = b[ks] - old
          C = max(b[:i].max(initial=0), b[min(2*n-1, i+n):].max(initial=0)) if any else 0.0
          # define f
          c0 = 1.0 - y
          def F(x):
             if x <0: x=0
             den = c0+x
             if den < 1e-12: return 1e12
             vals = base + coeff*x
             if quad.any(): vals[quad_idx]=base[quad_idx]+x*x
             m = C if C >= vals.max() else vals.max()
             return m/(den*den)
          # Brute grid to find bracket: sample x on [0, x_hi]
          # x_hi: start max(1.0, 10*y, 0.5)
          x_hi = min(1000.0, max(1.0, 10.0*y, (1.0-y)*9.0))  # if y small, 9 corresponds r=0.9
          # But if y=0, x_hi=9; if optimum x>10? likely not.
          # use minimize_scalar bounded [0, x_hi]
          res = minimize_scalar(F, bounds=(0.0, x_hi), method='bounded', options={'xatol':1e-6, 'maxiter':30})
          # Also evaluate grid ratios to be safe:
          candidates = [0, y*0.5, y, y*1.5, max(y*0.1,1e-6), ...] maybe.
```

Let's implement `fast_score` matching evaluator exactly for normalized arrays:
```
def fast_M(a):
    n=len(a)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: return inf
    b=np.convolve(a,a)
    M=b.max()
    return 2*n*M/(s*s)
```
But if a not normalized, fast_score same as evaluator except cap. Evaluator clips to 0,1000; we keep. Use `np.convolve` on float64. For length maybe up to 1024, conv O(1M) okay.
For coordinate F, M (without 2n). We maintain a sum=1.

**Coordinate_descent implementation details**:
```
def coordinate_descent(a, max_passes=20, deadline=None, random_order=False, min_time=1.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, float).copy()
    a = np.clip(a, 0, 1000)
    s = a.sum(); if s<0.01: return a
    a /= s
    n = len(a)
    b = np.convolve(a,a)
    M = b.max()
    best_a=a.copy(); best_M=M
    improved_passes=0
    for p in range(max_passes):
       if deadline and time.monotonic()>deadline: break
       order = rng.permutation(n) if random_order else np.arange(n)
       pass_improved=0
       for i in order:
          if deadline and time.monotonic()>deadline-1: break
          y=a[i]
          # Build arrays
          start=i; end=min(2*n-1, i+n)
          # affected ks from i to i+n-1 inclusive length n
          ks = np.arange(i, i+n)  # because i+n-1 <=2n-2 for i<=n-1, i+n <=2n-1 size n. Yes all within? If i=n-1, i+n-1=2n-2, i+n=2n-1 size n, last index 2n-2. Good.
          js = ks - i  # 0..n-1
          quad = (js == i)
          a_js = a[js]
          old = np.empty(n)
          old[~quad] = 2*y*a_js[~quad]
          old[quad] = y*y if quad.any()
          base = b[ks] - old
          C0 = max(b[:i].max(initial=0.0), b[i+n:].max(initial=0.0)) # b length 2n-1; b[i+n:] valid? For i=n-1, i+n=2n-1 -> empty; good.
          lin = ~quad
          if lin.any():
             lb=base[lin]; lc=2*a_js[lin]
          else: lb=None
          qb = base[quad].item() if quad.any() else None
          c0 = 1-y
          # if c0 <=0 and y==1: handle by grid maybe
          def F(x, lb=lb, lc=lc, qb=qb, c0=c0, C0=C0):
             x = float(x)
             if x < 0: x=0
             den = c0 + x
             if den <= 1e-15:
                return 1e30
             m = C0
             if lb is not None:
                # max of lb + lc*x
                mm = float(np.max(lb + lc*x))
                if mm > m: m = mm
             if qb is not None:
                mm = qb + x*x
                if mm > m: m = mm
             return m/(den*den)
          # initial x
          y_safe = y
          x_hi = min(50.0, max(1.0, 10.0*y, 9.0*(1.0 - y))) # 9 gives r=0.9
          # But if y=0, x_hi=9; if y=0.9, 1? 9*0.1=0.9, 10y=9, x_hi=9.
          # Evaluate current F(y)=M; also grid
          xs = np.array([0.0, x_hi])
          if y > 0:
             xs = np.concatenate((xs, y*np.array([0.01,0.05,0.2,0.5,0.8,1.0,1.2,1.5,2.0,3.0,5.0])))
          # add normalized-r mapping for r values if y not pathologically
          rvals = np.array([0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.7,0.9])
          # x = r*(1-y)/(1-r) if r<1
          xs = np.concatenate((xs, rvals*(1-y)/(1-rvals)))
          xs = np.clip(xs, 0, x_hi)
          # add linearly spaced maybe
          xs = np.unique(np.concatenate((xs, np.linspace(0,x_hi,15))))
          # Evaluate F on xs; find best_x
          fs = np.array([F(x) for x in xs])
          idx = int(np.nanargmin(fs))
          best_x = xs[idx]; best_f = fs[idx]
          # refine with minimize_scalar around best_x if not at bounds
          if best_x > 0 and best_x < x_hi:
             lo = max(0, best_x*0.5)
             hi = min(x_hi, best_x*2.0)
             try:
                res = minimize_scalar(F, bounds=(lo,hi), method='bounded', options={'xatol':1e-7, 'maxiter':50})
                if res.fun < best_f:
                   best_x=best.x; best_f=res.fun
             except: pass
          # Also refine full if grid best near boundaries? Maybe run full bounded once if best_f close? Full bounded may be expensive; but we can run if time per coordinate? 20 evals okay. Let's run full bounded if n<=600 and aborted? To save, run once per coordinate? Already grid+local 60 evals. Add full may 80. Fine.
          try:
             res = minimize_scalar(F, bounds=(0.0, x_hi), method='bounded', options={'xatol':1e-7, 'maxiter':50})
             if res.fun < best_f:
                best_x=best.x; best_f=res.fun
          except: pass
          if best_f < M * (1 - 1e-10) and (1-y+best_x) >= 0.01:
             # update a
             old_a = a.copy()? no need
             a[i] = best_x
             a /= a.sum()
             b = np.convolve(a,a)
             M = b.max()
             pass_improved += 1
             if M < best_M - 1e-12:
                best_M=M; best_a=a.copy()
       if pass_improved == 0:
          break
    return best_a
```

**LP update implementation**:
```
def lp_direction(a, M, factor=1.0):
   n=len(a)
   # build A sparse
   rows=[] cols=[] vals=[]
   for k in range(2*n-1):
      lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
      for j in range(lo,hi+1):
          rows.append(k); cols.append(j); vals.append(a[k-j])
   A=sps.csr_matrix((vals,(rows,cols)),shape=(2*n-1,n))
   b_ub=np.full(2*n-1, M*factor)
   c=-np.ones(n)
   res=linprog(c,A_ub=A,b_ub=b_ub,bounds=[(0,None)]*n,method='highs',options={'presolve':True,'time_limit':?})
```


**Subgradient implementation**:
```
def subgradient_step(a, max_steps=100, deadline=...):
  n=len(a); a=sum1; M=fast_M
  for step:
    b=np.convolve(a,a); M=b.max()
    # active top K with softmax
    K=min(64, max(8, int(0.2*len(b))))
    order=np.argsort(b)[-K:]
    w = (b[order]-M)/? softmax
    tau = max(1e-6, 0.1*M)
    w = np.exp((b[order]-M)/tau); w/=w.sum()
    d=np.zeros(n)
    for k,wk in zip(order,w):
       lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
       idx=np.arange(lo,hi+1)
       d[idx] += 2*wk*a[k-idx]
    # normalize direction
    d_mean? Since directions with sum zero better. We can subtract mean: d -= d.mean() (constant direction doesn't change b? Constant direction scaled? But sum constraint projection handles; subtract mean to reduce norm)
    dn=norm(d)
    if dn small break
    for eta in [0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4]:
       cand=a - eta*(d/dn)
       cand=np.clip(cand,0,None)
       s=cand.sum()
       if s<0.01: continue
       cand/=s
       val=fast_M(cand)
       if val < M*(1-1e-12): accept; break
    if not accept: random perturb or break
```
This may help escape coordinate minima. We can run after coordinate and after LP with random active K sizes.

**LP time and matrix cache**:
Building sparse A each LP can be optimized with precomputed indices for n. Function `buildA(a)` loop over k,j; for n=320, loop 102k Python, maybe 0.01s? Actually 100k Python loops ~0.01-0.03s. Could be okay. For many LPs, maybe 1s. We can precompute row,col index pattern once per n:
```
def conv_indices(n):
   rows=np.empty(n*n,dtype=np.int32); cols=...; count=0
   for k in... for j... fill
   return rows,cols,count
# A = sps.csr_matrix((a[?], (rows,cols)), shape) but vals depend on a[k-j], not directly a indexing? Could precompute for each entry (k,j), the value is a[k-j]. We can precompute `val_idx = k-j` array. Then vals = a[val_idx] each time. Good.
```

```
def gen_candidates(n):
    if n<2: return [np.ones(n)]
    out=[]
    out.append(np.ones(n)/n)
    r=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    for alpha in [0.1,0.3,0.5,0.7,0.85,0.95,0.99]:
       a=np.clip(1+alpha*np.cos(np.pi*r),1e-8,None)
       out.append(a/a.sum())
    for q in [2,4,8]:
       for alpha in [0.3,0.7,1.2,2.0]:
           a=np.clip(1+alpha*np.power(1-2*r,q),1e-10,None) # even q positive; at center 1
           out.append(a/a.sum())
    for w in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5]:
       for alpha in [0.5,1,2,4]:
           a=1+alpha*np.exp(-(2*np.abs(r-0.5))/w)
           out.append(a/a.sum())
    # endpoint spikes
    u=np.ones(n)/n
    for ratio in [0.5,1,2,5,10,20]:
       a=u.copy(); a[0]=u[0]*ratio; a[-1]=u[-1]*ratio; out.append(a/a.sum())
    # random symmetric
    for sigma in [0.2,0.5,1.0,1.5]:
       a=np.abs(rng.normal(1.0, sigma, n)) + 1e-8
       a=0.5*(a+a[::-1])
       out.append(a/a.sum())
       a=np.exp(sigma*rng.standard_normal(n))
       a=0.5*(a+a[::-1])
       out.append(a/a.sum())
    return out
```
This many (~40). Evaluating all fast maybe okay. But for n=512, conv 40*262k=10M, okay. Could reduce.

```
def interp(a, n):
    if a.size==n: return a.copy()/a.sum()
    ox=np.linspace(0,1,a.size); nx=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    out=np.interp(nx, ox, a)
    out[out<0]=0; normalize
```

**LP `lp_step`**:

```
def lp_improve(a, stop, max_solves=10):
    a=normalize; M=b.max(); best=a; bestM=M
    factors=[1.0,1.01,1.02,1.05,1.1]
    solve_count=0
    while time.monotonic()<stop and solve_count<max_solves:
        for f in factors:
           g=solve_lp(a, currentM, f)
           if g not None:
              # line search
              best_t=None
              for t in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.7,1.0,1.2? but if t>1 not convex; use 1]
                 cand=(1-t)*a + t*g
                 val=fast_M(cand)
                 if val<bestM*(1-1e-12): accept
              if accepted: update a,b,M,best; break factors; continue outer
              # also try g alone? t=1 included
        solve_count+
        if no improvement in all factors: break maybe after perturbation
    return best
```

```
# We can remove rows where A.sum(axis=1) <=0? if row all zero (all a[j]? Actually if a all zero row maybe, but a sum1 so every row has some? For k near edges, if a entries corresponding zero, row sum zero; constraint unconstrained. Highs okay.

   if 'time_limit' not? options['time_limit']=3
   res=linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A,b_ub=bub,bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs', options=options)
   if res.success and res.x is not None:
       g=np.clip(res.x,0,None); s=g.sum(); if s>0: return g/s

```

```
def get_pattern(n):
   if in cache return
   total=n*n
   rows=np.empty(total,np.int32); cols=np.empty(total,np.int32); validx=np.empty(total,np.int32)
   cnt=0
   for k in range(2*n-1):
      lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
      for j in range(lo,hi+1):
          rows[cnt]=k; cols[cnt]=j; validx[cnt]=k-j; cnt+=1
   # cnt should equal n*n? Let's check: Sum over k len = n^2? For n=3: k len1,2,3,2,1 total9 = n^2. Yes.
   return (rows,cols,validx)
```

Pseudo:
```
def deep_optimize(a, stop):
    a=normalize
    best=a; bestM=fast
    # initial coordinate to time min(120, stop-...)
    coord_stop = min(time.monotonic()+120, stop-60)
    a,b,M = coordinate_descent(a, stop=coord_stop, random_order=False)
    record(a)
    # remaining loop
    phase=0
    while time.monotonic() < stop - 30:
       if phase % 3 ==0:
          a = coordinate_descent(a, max_passes=2, stop=min(time+30, stop-30))
          record(a)
       elif phase %3 ==1:
          if linprog:
             a2 = lp_improve(a, stop=min(time+90, stop-30), max_solves=8)
             record(a2); if improved: a=a2
          else: fallback continue
       else:
          a2 = subgradient(a, stop=min(time+30, stop-30), max_steps=100)
          record(a2); if improved: a=a2
       phase+=1
    # final coordinate polish to stop-5
    a=coordinate_descent(a, stop=stop-5, max_passes=10)
    record(a)
    return a
```
Need coordinate_descent returns best within from its start; if it receives a and stops, returns best. It uses `a` current. If no improvement, returns input.

`coordinate_descent` should not record exact? It can update internal best. We'll record outside.

`lp_improve` returns best a after LP line searches and coordinate polish? It can do own coordinate small after each accepted LP. We'll implement inside.

`subgradient` returns best after steps, maybe coordinate polish? Could call separate.

```
best_score = float('inf'); best_arr = None
best_fast = inf; best_fast_arr = None

def fast_score(a):
    try:
      a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
      if a.size==0: return inf
      a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
      # finite
      s=a.sum()
      if s <= 1e-15: return inf
      b=np.convolve(a,a)
      M=b.max()
      if not np.isfinite(M): return inf
      return float(2.0*a.size*float(M)/(s*s))
    except: return inf
```
`normalize`:
```
def norm_a(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    np.nan_to_num(a, copy=False, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s=a.sum()
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15: return None
    if s < 0.01:
       a = a/s*0.01
       s=0.01
    # cap if max >1000
    if a.max() > 1000.0:
       a = a / a.max() * 999.0
       s = a.sum()
       if s <=1e-15: return None
    return a/s  # sum 1? For record, maybe not; for optimization sum1.
```

`record(a)`:
```
def record(a):
   nonlocal best_score,best_arr
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if a.size==0: return inf
   a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   s=a.sum()
   if s < 0.01:
       if s>0: a = a/s*0.01
       else: return inf
   vals=[float(x) for x in a]
   v=evaluate_sequence(vals)
   if math.isfinite(v):
      if v < best_score - 1e-15:
          best_score=v; best_arr=a.copy()
   return v
```

```
def anneal(a, stop, iters=200):
   current=a; M=fast; best=a; bestM=M
   for it in range(iters):
     if time > stop: break
     sigma = max(0.02, 0.3*(stop-time)/(stop))
     if rng.random()<0.5:
        cand=current * np.exp(sigma*rng.standard_normal(n)); cand=clean
     else:
        i,j=rng.integers(0,n,2); t=rng.choice([0.1,0.3,0.5,0.7,1.0])
        cand=current.copy(); ... transfer
     f=fast_score(cand)
     if f < M*(1-1e-12): current=cand; M=f; update best; M? else maybe if no improve many, set current=best
   return best
```

```
def make_B(n, shape, cap_factor=1.2):
   L=2*n-1
   M_target=target/(2*n)
   cap = M_target*cap_factor
   avg = 1/L
   # shape normalized to sum1 and zero mean? 
   shape = np.asarray(shape,float); shape=shape - shape.mean()
   if abs(shape.max())>0: shape/=shape.max()
   for gamma in [0,0.1,...,1.0]:
       b = avg + gamma*shape
       b=np.clip(b,0.0,cap)
       s=b.sum()
       if s>0:
           b/=s
           if b.max() <= cap*(1+1e-6): return b
   # fallback: cap profile
   b = np.clip(shape,0,None)
   if b.sum()==0: b=np.ones(L)/L
   b = np.minimum(b, cap)
   # if max after normalization >cap, add floor? Do iterative blend with flat
   best = np.ones(L)/L
   for t in linspace(0,1,20):
      c = (1-t)*flat + t*clip(shape,0,cap); c/=c.sum(); if c.max()<=cap: return c
   return flat
```

```
def make_B(n, shape, cap_factor):
    L=2*n-1
    avg=1.0/L
    cap = max(avg*1.000001, (TARGET/(2*n))*cap_factor)
    shape=np.asarray(shape,float)
    if not finite or all zero: return np.full(L, avg)
    # center shape to zero mean and scale
    shape = shape - shape.mean()
    smax = np.max(np.abs(shape))
    if smax == 0: return flat
    shape = shape / smax  # range [-1,1]
    # Try mix with flat: b = 1 - t + t*shape? ensure positive
```

Waterfill algorithm:
```
def cap_profile(shape, cap):
   L=...; cap=max(cap, avg+1e-9)
   # shape positive? We can use shape as desired priorities. Set b = np.clip(shape,0,cap).
   # If sum b >=1, normalize? Normalizing may exceed cap if sum<1? Actually if sum>=1, b/=s (s>1) lowers, okay. If sum<1, need add 1-sum mass to entries with b<cap.
   if b.sum() > 0 and b.sum() >=1:
       b/=b.sum(); return b
   remaining=1-b.sum()
   # add to entries with room, cycling
   room=cap-b
   # distribute remaining evenly across room: avg_add = remaining / (room.sum())
   add = min(remaining / (room.sum()+1e-12), 1) * room? If cap-add? Simpler:
   add_level = remaining / room.sum()
   b_new = np.minimum(b + add_level*room, cap)
   # due to saturation, iterate a few times
   for _ in range(10):
      rem=1-b_new.sum()
      if rem <= 1e-12: break
      room=cap-b_new
      total_room=room.sum()
      if total_room < rem: return None # cap total <1
      b_new += rem*(room/total_room)
      b_new=np.minimum(b_new,cap)
   b_new/=b_new.sum()
   return b_new if b_new.max()<=cap*1.0001 else None
```
This gives a distribution with max cap and sum1, biased to shape. Good. Use shape positive. For shape with negative, clip to 0. If shape all negative, flat.

```
def root_candidates(n, max_count=50):
   L=2*n-1
   r=np.linspace(0,1,L)
   shapes=[]
   d=2*r-1
   shapes.append(np.ones(L))
   shapes.append(1+np.cos(np.pi*r)) # high ends
   shapes.append(1+0.7*np.cos(np.pi*r))
   shapes.append(1-d*d) # high center? at ends 0, center 1
   shapes.append(1+d*d) # center 1, ends 2? Actually high center/ends? d^2 high ends.
   shapes.append(np.exp(-d*d/0.2)) # center high
   shapes.append(np.exp(-d*d/0.5))
   shapes.append(np.exp(-2*r*r)+np.exp(-2*(1-r)**2)) # high ends
   shapes.append(np.exp(-2*(r-0.25)**2)+np.exp(-2*(r-0.75)**2)) # two peaks
   shapes.append(1/(1+5*d*d)) center high
   shapes.append(1/(1+5*r*(1-r)))? r*(1-r)=0 ends, high center; invert high ends: 1/(1+5*r*(1-r))? at center denominator 1.25 lower, ends 1 => ends higher.
   for shape in shapes:
     for cf in [1.0,1.05,1.1,1.2,1.5,2.0]:
        b=cap_profile(n, shape, TARGET/(2*n)*cf)
        if b is None: continue
        a=root_from_B(b,n)
        if a is not None: yield a
        if count > max_count: break
```

```
def cap_profile(n, shape, cap):
    L=2*n-1
    avg=1.0/L
    if cap < avg*(1-1e-12): cap=avg*(1+1e-9)
    shape=np.asarray(shape,float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(shape)): shape=np.where(np.isfinite(shape),0,shape)
    shape = shape - shape.min()  # make nonnegative priorities
    # if flat, return flat
    if shape.max() <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(L, avg)
    # scale shape to sum 1? Not necessary but for distribution.
    shape_sum=shape.sum()
    if shape_sum<=0: return flat
    shape = shape / shape_sum  # desired proportions
    # Start with b = min(shape_normalized * L? We need sum 1. We'll do waterfill with priority q.
    # We want allocate 1 mass to L variables with cap, desired proportional to q. Equivalent b_i = min(cap, λ q_i). Find λ.
    q=shape
    # total cap = L*cap >=1
    # Find λ such that sum min(cap, q_i λ)=1.
    # Since q nonneg, cap_profile can do bisection λ.
    lo=0; hi=cap/min(q.max(),?)? Simpler: b = q; if sum>=1: normalize? But if q sum=1, b=q may have max>cap. Use min(cap, cap? ). Bisection:
    max_q=q.max()
    if max_q<=0: flat
    # max lambda such that lambda*max_q = cap => lambda_hi = cap/max_q
    # increase lambda until sum min(cap, λ q)=1. At λ_hi, sum min(cap, λ_hi q)=sum min(cap, cap q/qmax) may be less than1? Since capacity total >1, at λ hi maybe sum? For fine, if max q huge, min gives some at cap and others small; sum could be less than1? But we can further increase lambda? No lamb hi already makes largest at cap; increasing would not increase capped, but uncapped increase. Wait if lambda>hi, largest remains cap, others λq. So hi not upper for sum. λ max such that uncapped sum maybe; as λ→∞ sum→ total cap (L*cap) >1, so feasible. We can bisection lo=0, hi= max(1/cap, 1.0, 10.0/max_q) maybe until sum>=1.
    def sum_at(lam): return np.sum(np.minimum(cap, lam*q))
    hi = max(1.0, 1.0/max_q) # if q sum=1, λ=1 gives sum1 if no cap; if cap small, need hi bigger
    while sum_at(hi) < 1.0:
        hi *= 2
        if hi>1e12: break
    for _ in range(60):
        mid=(lo+hi)/2
        s=sum_at(mid)
        if s<1: lo=mid
        else: hi=mid
    lam=hi
    b=np.minimum(cap, lam*q)
    # adjust for numerical sum
    s=b.sum()
    if s>0:
       # if s<1? add room
       if s < 1-1e-12:
          rem=1-s
          room=cap-b
          total_room=room.sum()
          if total_room < rem-1e-12:
             return None
          b += rem*(room/total_room)
          b=np.minimum(b,cap)
       elif s > 1+1e-12:
          b/=s
          b=np.minimum(b,cap)
          # if b max slightly >cap? no
       return b
```

```
def subgradient_optimize(a, stop, max_steps=200):
   a=normalize; n=a.size; M=fast_M; best=a; bestM=M
   step=0
   while time<stop and step<max_steps:
      b=np.convolve(a,a); M=float(b.max())
      if M < bestM: bestM=M; best=a.copy(); update_fast
      K = min(len(b), max(16, n//4))? L=2n-1. Use K = min(L, max(16, n//2))? For n=320, K=160 maybe many. We want top active. Use K in [16,32,64,128] cycles.
      order=np.argsort(b)[-K:]
      vals=b[order]
      tau=max(1e-9, 0.05*(vals.max()-vals.min()+1e-12))
      w=np.exp((vals-vals.max())/tau); w/=w.sum()
      d=zeros
      for idx,wk in zip(order,w): ...
      d -= d.mean()
      dn=np.linalg.norm(d)
      if dn < 1e-14: break
      d=d/dn
      accepted=False
      for eta in [1e-4,3e-4,1e-3,3e-3,1e-2,3e-2,1e-1,3e-1]:
          cand=a - eta*d
          cand=np.clip(cand,0,None); s=cand.sum(); if s<=1e-12: continue
          cand/=s
          f=fast_score(cand)
          if f < M*(1-1e-10):
              a=cand; M=f; update; accepted=True; break
      if not accepted:
          # try perturb
          cand=a*(1+0.05*rng.standard_normal(n)); normalize; if fast<M: a=cand
          stale+=1; if stale>20: break
      step+=1
   return best
```
Because normalized d can be large; eta grid maybe enough. We can scale eta by a.mean(): `eta_scale = a.mean()` (≈1/n). If d norm normalized, direction components ~1/sd; Actually d normalized by norm, components ~1/sqrt(n). Subtract eta*dir changes a by eta/sqrt(n). To change weights ~1/n, eta ~1/sqrt(n). For n=320, 0.05. Our eta up to0.3 good. Maybe `eta` should be relative. We can use grid `base_eta = max(1e-3, a.mean()/0.1)` etc. Simpler: after d normalized to max abs 1? Use `d /= d.max()` then components ~1. Then eta around a values ~0.003. Use grid [1e-5,1e-4,3e-4,1e-3,3e-3,1e-2,3e-2]. **Anneal / random transfers (optional)**:
```
def random_improve(a, stop, max_iter=300):
   a=normalize; n=a.size; M=fast; best=a; bestM=M; stale=0
   for it in range(max_iter):
      if time>stop: break
      r=rng.random()
      if r<0.5:
         sigma=0.05+0.3*(time-left/maxinitial?) maybe 0.1
         cand=a*np.exp(sigma*rng.standard_normal(n)); clean
      elif r<0.8:
         i,j=rng.integers(0,n,2); if i==j continue
         t=rng.choice([0.1,0.3,0.5,0.7,1.0])
         cand=a.copy(); old_i=cand[i]; old_j=cand[j]; cand[i]=(1-t)*old_i + t*old_j; cand[j]=t*old_i+(1-t)*old_j
      else:
         # block scale: choose half intervals low/high
         mask=rng.random(n)<0.1; cand=a; cand[mask]*=rng.choice([0.5,2])
      f=fast_score(cand)
      if f < M*(1-1e-12): a=cand; M=f; update; stale=0
      else:
         stale+=1; if stale>20: # reset to best
            a=best.copy(); M=bestM
            if stale>50: break
   return best
```
Probably not needed but can use leftover.

**Deep optimize sequence** should not call record_exact every few steps because exact maybe slow. We'll update fast inside. At end, caller records exact. During deep, if fast improves, update global fast. If exact hidden same, fine.

Detailed code draft:

```
import numpy as np
import time, math
try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize_scalar
    import scipy.sparse as sps
except Exception:
    linprog=None; minimize_scalar=None; sps=None

_CENTER_CACHE={}
def _conv_pattern(n):
   key=int(n)
   if key in _CENTER_CACHE: return _CENTER_CACHE[key]
   total=key*key
   rows=np.empty(total,dtype=np.int32); cols=np.empty(total,dtype=np.int32); vals=np.empty(total,dtype=np.int32)
   cnt=0
   for k in range(2*key-1):
      lo=max(0,k-key+1); hi=min(key-1,k)
      for j in range(lo,hi+1):
          rows[cnt]=k; cols[cnt]=j; vals[cnt]=k-j; cnt+=1
   if cnt != total:
      rows=rows[:cnt]; cols=cols[:cnt]; vals=vals[:cnt]
   _CENTER_CACHE[key]=(rows,cols,vals)
   return _CENTER_CACHE[key]
```

`to_sum1`:
```
def to_sum1(a):
    try:
       arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except: return None
    if arr.size==0: return None
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    s=float(arr.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300: return None
    arr = arr / s
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)): return None
    if arr.max() > 1000: arr = arr/arr.max()*999
    if arr.sum() <=1e-300: return None
    arr /= arr.sum()
    return arr
```
If sum<0.01 originally but positive, normalizing to sum1 gives valid. Good.

`fast_score` uses to_sum1? It can normalize internally to use M score; but if a sum not1, score invariant. Use sum to avoid overflow. If a values >1000? We clip. Use `a=to_sum1(a)`; if None inf; then b=np.convolve. But to_sum1 clips and sum1, good. However fast_score called very often; to_sum1 overhead. We can assume arrays normalized; but for safety implement:
```
def fast_score(a):
   arr=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   if arr.size==0: return inf
   arr=np.clip(np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0,posinf=1000,neginf=0),0,1000)
   s=float(arr.sum())
   if s <= 1e-15: return inf
   b=np.convolve(arr,arr)
   M=float(b.max())
   if not math.isfinite(M): return inf
   return float(2.0*arr.size*M/(s*s))
```
This avoids normalizing; if arr not sum1, fine. In coordinate, arr sum1.

`interpolate`:
```
def interpolate(arr,n):
   arr=to_sum1(arr)
   if arr is None: return np.ones(n)/n
   if arr.size==n: return arr.copy()
   if arr.size<2: return np.full(n,1.0/n)
   ox=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,arr.size); nx=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   out=np.interp(nx,ox,arr)
   out=np.clip(out,0,None)
   s=out.sum()
   if s<=1e-300: return np.ones(n)/n
   return out/s
```

`cap_profile`:
```
def cap_profile(n, shape, mcap):
   L=2*n-1
   avg=1.0/L
   mcap=float(mcap)
   if mcap <=0 or not math.isfinite(mcap): return np.full(L,avg)
   if mcap < avg: mcap = avg*(1+1e-7)
   shape=np.asarray(shape,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if shape.size != L: shape=np.resize(shape,L)
   shape=np.where(np.isfinite(shape),shape,0.0)
   shape = shape - np.min(shape)
   if shape.max() <= 1e-300:
       return np.full(L,avg)
   q=shape/shape.sum()
   # waterfill b=min(cap, lambda q)
   # We want sum=1. Find lambda by bisection.
   def sum_at(lam, q=q):
       return float(np.sum(np.minimum(mcap, lam*q)))
   # if q.sum()=1, lambda=1 gives sum<=1 if caps? Actually min <= q, sum<=1; need maybe lambda>1.
   hi = max(1.0, mcap/ (q.max()+1e-300)) # not correct if capped; use 1.0 maybe enough? If q sum=1 and cap small, lambda>1 maybe.
   # ensure hi sum >=1
   for _ in range(80):
       s= sum_at(hi)
       if s >= 1.0: break
       hi *= 2.0
       if hi > 1e12: break
   if s < 1.0: # capacity insufficient? But total cap = L*mcap >=? mcap>avg => L*mcap>1, so should reach.
       # fallback uniform cap
       rem=1-avg*L? no
   lo=0.0
   for _ in range(80):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       if sum_at(mid) < 1.0: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
   b=np.minimum(mcap, hi*q)
   # sum should ~1
   bsum=float(b.sum())
   if bsum <=0: return flat
   if bsum < 1-1e-12:
       rem=1-bsum
       room=mcap-b
       total_room=float(room.sum())
       if total_room < rem - 1e-12:
           # fallback add to flat
           b=np.full(L,avg)
           return b
       b += rem*(room/total_room)
       b=np.minimum(b,mcap)
   elif bsum > 1+1e-12:
       b /= bsum
       b = np.minimum(b,mcap)
       # if after min sum <1, add
       s2=float(b.sum())
       if s2 < 1-1e-12:
          rem=1-s2; room=mcap-b; tr=room.sum(); b += rem*(room/tr); b=np.minimum(b,mcap)
   # final normalize
   s3=float(b.sum())
   if s3>0: b/=s3
   return b
```
Need check if `hi` initial and `s` defined if loop not run? We'll set `s=sum_at(hi)`. If hi too small after loop? capacity enough. Good.

`root_from_B`:
```
def root_from_B(b,n):
    if b.size != 2*n-1: return None
    b=np.asarray(b,float)
    if b[0] <=0: return None
    a=np.zeros(n)
    a0=math.sqrt(float(b[0]))
    if a0 < 1e-12: return None
    a[0]=a0
    for k in range(1,n):
        s=float(b[k])
        if k>1:
            # sum i=1 to k-1 a[i]*a[k-i]
            s -= float(np.dot(a[1:k], a[:k-1][::-1]))
        val=s/(2*a0)
        if val < -1e-7:
            return None
        if val < 0: val=0.0
        a[k]=val
    a=np.clip(a,0,None)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <=1e-12: return None
    return a/s
```
Wait recurrence should include terms where k-i >= n? For k<n, all i 1..k-1 valid. Good.
But `a[:k-1][::-1]` length k-1, `a[1:k]` length k-1. For k=2, dot a[1:2] with a[:1][::-1] = a1*a0? But sum i=1 to k-1=1 => a1*a_{1}? Wait a[k-i] with i=1: a1. The term in b_2 = a0*a2 + a1*a1 + a2*a0. Recurrence subtract ∑_{i=1}^{k-1} a_i a_{k-i} includes i=1: a1*a1. My dot a[1:k] (a1) and a[:k-1][::-1] (a0) gives a1*a0, wrong. Need sum i=1 to k-1: a_i * a_{k-i}. For k=2, a1*a1. For k=3: a1*a2 + a2*a1 =2a1a2. Vectors `a[1:k]` length k-1: indices 1..k-1. Need `a[k-1:0:-1]` indices k-1 down to 1. So `a[1:k]` and `a[k-1:0:-1]`. For k=2: a[1:2]=a1, a[1:0:-1]? slice `a[k-1:0:-1]` with k-1=1 -> a[1:0:-1] = [a1]? yes. Good. For k=3: a[1:3]=[a1,a2], a[2:0:-1]=[a2,a1] -> dot a1a2+a2a1. Good. So use `a[k-1:0:-1]` not `a[:k-1][::-1]`. Correct.
But terms where i=k/2 double? The sum over i including both i and k-i gives two terms, which matches convolution coefficient includes both. For k even, if i=k/2 appears once in sum i=1..k-1, but actual b_k has a_{k/2}^2 once. Wait recurrence: b_k = 2a0 a_k + ∑_{i=1}^{k-1} a_i a_{k-i}. For k=2, sum i=1 to1: a1*a1 = a1^2. Good (one term). For k=3 sum i=1,2: a1a2+a2a1=2a1a2. Actual: a0a3+a1a2+a2a1+a3a0, yes. Good.
So recurrence correct.

But if k-i > n-1? for k<n no.

After root, actual square first n coefficients? For k<n, yes (using b). The recurrence uses b[0..n-1]. Good.

`root_candidates`:
```
def root_candidates(n, max_count=60, max_time=None):
    L=2*n-1
    r=np.linspace(0,1,L)
    d=2*r-1
    shapes=[]
    shapes.append(np.ones(L))
    shapes.append(1.0 + 1.0*np.cos(np.pi*r))
    shapes.append(1.0 + 0.7*np.cos(np.pi*r))
    shapes.append(1.0 - 1.0*np.cos(np.pi*r)) # zero ends, high center?
    shapes.append(1.0 + 0.7*d*d)
    shapes.append(1.0 - 0.5*d*d)
    shapes.append(np.exp(-d*d/0.1))
    shapes.append(np.exp(-d*d/0.5))
    shapes.append(np.exp(-2*r*r) + np.exp(-2*(1-r)**2))
    shapes.append(np.exp(-2*(r-0.25)**2) + np.exp(-2*(r-0.75)**2))
    shapes.append(1.0/(1.0 + 3.0*d*d))
    shapes.append(1.0 + 3.0*r*(1-r)) # center high
    shapes.append(1.0 + 3.0*(r*(1-r))**2)
    out=[]
    target_m = TARGET/(2.0*n)
    cf_list=[1.0,1.02,1.05,1.1,1.2,1.5,2.0]
    for sh in shapes:
      for cf in cf_list:
         if time.monotonic() > t0+90: break  # optional but inside use stop param
         b=cap_profile(n, sh, target_m*cf)
         if b is None: continue
         a=root_from_B(b,n)
         if a is not None:
             a=to_sum1(a)
             if a is not None: out.append(a)
         if len(out)>=max_count: return out
    return out
```
Use no global stop; caller time. To limit, pass `stop_gen`.

`gen_candidates(n, stop_gen)`:
```
def gen_candidates(n, stop_gen):
   out=[]
   if time.monotonic() >= stop_gen: return out
   u=np.ones(n)/n
   out.append(u)
   # root candidates
   out.extend(root_candidates(n, max_count=40, stop_gen=stop_gen))
   if time.monotonic() >= stop_gen: return out
   r=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   for alpha in [0.2,0.5,0.8,0.95]:
       a=np.clip(1+alpha*np.cos(np.pi*r),1e-12,None); out.append(a/a.sum())
   for q in [2,4,8]:
      for alpha in [0.5,1.0,2.0]:
         a=np.clip(1+alpha*np.power(2*r-1,q),1e-12,None); out.append(a/a.sum())
   # endpoint spikes
   for ratio in [1,2,5,10]:
      a=u.copy(); a[0]=u[0]*ratio; a[-1]=u[-1]*ratio; out.append(a/a.sum())
   # random
   k=0
   for sigma in [0.2,0.5,1.0]:
      for _ in range(3):
         a=np.abs(rng.normal(1,sigma,n))+1e-8
         a=0.5*(a+a[::-1])
         out.append(a/a.sum())
         a=np.exp(sigma*rng.standard_normal(n))+1e-8
         a=0.5*(a+a[::-1])
         out.append(a/a.sum())
         k+=1
   return out
```
This many. To avoid too many for large n, in initial loop we can limit by stopping when time. The candidates not filtered by count until pool. But root 60, shapes ~30, total 100 per length. For 5 lengths, 500 candidates. Fast scores each O(n^2) could be heavy: 500*512^2=130M, okay maybe several seconds. Root computation likely more. Use generation stop 90s, enough.
Maybe reduce root max_count to 25 per length to save.

`deep_optimize`:
```
def deep_optimize(a, stop):
    a=to_sum1(a); if None return a
    n=a.size
    current=a.copy(); best=a.copy(); bestM=fast_score(a); update_fast
    # initial coordinate
    coord_stop = min(stop-60, time.monotonic()+180)
    a=coordinate_descent(a, max_passes=8, stop=coord_stop)
    f=current fast? coordinate updates best_fast; compare
    if fast_score(current) >= fast_score(a): current=a
    best=a; bestM=fast_score(a); record? no
    # sym
    sym=0.5*(a+a[::-1]); sym=sym/sym.sum(); if fast_score(sym)<=bestM: a=sym; best=a; bestM=...
    # loop remaining
    phase=0
    while time.monotonic() < stop-20:
       if phase==0:
          stop2=min(time.monotonic()+90, stop-20)
          a2=coordinate_descent(a, max_passes=4, stop=stop2)
          f=fast_score(a2)
          if f<=bestM: a=a2; best=a2; bestM=f; update_fast
       elif phase==1:
          if linprog is not None and n <= 700:
             stop2=min(time.monotonic()+120, stop-20)
             a2=lp_improve(a, stop=stop2, max_solves=6)
             f=fast_score(a2)
             if f < bestM: a=a2; best=a2; bestM=f; update_fast
          else:
             # fallback random/subgradient
             pass
       elif phase==2:
          stop2=min(time.monotonic()+60, stop-20)
          a2=subgradient_optimize(a, stop=stop2, max_steps=80)
          f=fast_score(a2)
          if f<bestM: a=a2; best=a2; bestM=f; update_fast
       else:
          stop2=min(time.monotonic()+40, stop-20)
          a2=random_improve(a, stop=stop2, max_iter=100)
          f=fast_score(a2)
          if f<bestM: a=a2; best=a2; bestM=f; update_fast
          # sym maybe
          sym=...
       phase=(phase+1)%4
    # final coord
    a=coordinate_descent(a, max_passes=3, stop=stop-5)
    f=fast_score(a); if f<bestM: best=a; bestM=f; update_fast
    return best
```
Need ensure `bestM` gets updated when `coordinate_descent` updates `best_fast` but we local best. We can after each function call compute `f=fast_score(a2)`. Update. `coordinate_descent` already updates global fast if M improves, but local best keep.

```
def lp_improve(a, stop, max_solves=10):
    a=to_sum1(a); if None return a
    n=a.size; if n<8 or linprog is None or sps is None: return a
    current=a.copy(); curM=fast_score(current); best=current; bestM=curM; update_fast
    for solve in range(max_solves):
       if time.monotonic() >= stop: break
       b=np.convolve(current,current); M=float(b.max())
       factors=[1.0,1.005,1.01,1.02,1.05]
       improved=False
       for fct in factors:
          if time.monotonic() >= stop: break
          g=solve_lp(current, M, fct)
          if g is None: continue
          for t in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.7,1.0]:
              cand=(1-t)*current + t*g
              cand=np.clip(cand,0,None); s=cand.sum(); if s<1e-12: continue
              cand/=s
              f=fast_score(cand)
              if f < bestM*(1-1e-12):
                  current=cand; bestM=f; best=cand.copy(); update_fast(current,f); improved=True; break
          if improved: break
       if not improved:
          # small random perturb then solve? maybe break
          break
       # after accepted, maybe coordinate polish 1 pass
       if time.monotonic() < stop-30:
          current=coordinate_descent(current, max_passes=2, stop=min(time.monotonic()+30, stop))
          f=fast_score(current); if f<bestM: bestM=f; best=current.copy(); update_fast
    return best
```

`solve_lp` nested. It uses `pattern`.
```
def solve_lp(a, M, factor):
   n=a.size
   rows,cols,idxp=_conv_pattern(n)
   vals=a[idxp]
   A=sps.csr_matrix((vals,(rows,cols)),shape=(2*n-1,n))
   bub=np.full(2*n-1, M*factor, dtype=float)
   c=-np.ones(n)
   opts={'presolve': True}
   # Maybe set time_limit
   try:
      res=linprog(c, A_ub=A,b_ub=bub,bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n, method='highs', options=opts)
   except Exception:
      # try with time limit option
      try:
         opts2=dict(opts); opts2['time_limit']=5
         res=linprog(c,A_ub=A,b_ub=bub,bounds=..., method='highs', options=opts2)
      except: return None
   if res is None: return None
   if getattr(res,'success',False) or getattr(res,'status',1)==0:
       x=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=float)
       if x.size==n:
          x=np.clip(x,0,None); s=x.sum(); if s>1e-12: return x/s
   return None
```
If `options['time_limit']` unsupported, first call will error; second uses it. Good.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize_scalar
    import scipy.sparse as sps
except Exception:
    linprog = None
    minimize_scalar = None
    sps = None

_PATTERN_CACHE = {}


def _conv_pattern(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n in _PATTERN_CACHE:
        return _PATTERN_CACHE[n]
    total = n * n
    rows = np.empty(total, dtype=np.int32)
    cols = np.empty(total, dtype=np.int32)
    vals = np.empty(total, dtype=np.int32)
    cnt = 0
    for k in range(2 * n - 1):
        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        hi = min(n - 1, k)
        for j in range(lo, hi + 1):
            rows[cnt] = k
            cols[cnt] = j
            vals[cnt] = k - j
            cnt += 1
    if cnt != total:
        rows = rows[:cnt]
        cols = cols[:cnt]
        vals = vals[:cnt]
    _PATTERN_CACHE[n] = (rows, cols, vals)
    return (rows, cols, vals)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = max(10.0, float(budget_s))
    except Exception:
        budget = 1300.0
    deadline = t0 + budget - 30.0
    TARGET = 1.50274349

    best_score = float("inf")
    best_arr = None
    best_fast = float("inf")
    best_fast_arr = None

    def finite(f):
        return math.isfinite(f)

    def to_sum1(a):
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        except Exception:
            return None
        if arr.size == 0:
            return None
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(arr.sum())
        if not finite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            return None
        arr = arr / s
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
            return None
        if arr.max() > 1000.0:
            arr = (arr / arr.max()) * 999.0
            s = float(arr.sum())
            if s <= 1e-300:
                return None
            arr = arr / s
        return arr

    def fast_score(a):
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if arr.size == 0:
                return float("inf")
            arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
            arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
            s = float(arr.sum())
            if s <= 1e-300:
                return float("inf")
            b = np.convolve(arr, arr)
            m = float(np.max(b))
            if not finite(m):
                return float("inf")
            return float(2.0 * arr.size * m / (s * s))
        except Exception:
            return float("inf")

    def update_fast(a, f=None):
        nonlocal best_fast, best_fast_arr
        try:
            arr = to_sum1(a)
            if arr is None:
                return
            if f is None:
                f = fast_score(arr)
            if finite(f) and f < best_fast:
                best_fast = float(f)
                best_fast_arr = arr.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    def record_exact(a):
        nonlocal best_score, best_arr
        try:
            arr = to_sum1(a)
            if arr is None:
                return float("inf")
            vals = [float(x) for x in arr]
            v = evaluate_sequence(vals)
            if finite(v) and v < best_score:
                best_score = float(v)
                best_arr = arr.copy()
            return float(v) if finite(v) else float("inf")
        except Exception:
            return float("inf")

    def record_candidate(a):
        try:
            arr = to_sum1(a)
            if arr is None:
                return float("inf")
            f = fast_score(arr)
            update_fast(arr, f)
            record_exact(arr)
            return f
        except Exception:
            return float("inf")

    def interpolate(a, n):
        try:
            src = to_sum1(a)
            n = int(n)
            if n <= 0:
                return np.ones(1)
            if src is None:
                return np.ones(n) / n
            if src.size == n:
                return src.copy()
            if src.size < 2:
                return np.ones(n) / n
            ox = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, src.size)
            nx = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
            out = np.interp(nx, ox, src)
            out = np.clip(out, 0.0, None)
            s = float(out.sum())
            if s <= 1e-300:
                return np.ones(n) / n
            return out / s
        except Exception:
            return np.ones(max(1, int(n))) / max(1, int(n))

    parent = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            p = to_sum1(h)
            if p is not None:
                parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        record_candidate(parent)
    else:
        fallback = np.ones(320) / 320.0
        record_candidate(fallback)

    def cap_profile(n, shape, mcap):
        L = 2 * n - 1
        avg = 1.0 / L
        mcap = float(mcap)
        if not finite(mcap) or mcap <= 0.0:
            return np.full(L, avg)
        if mcap < avg:
            mcap = avg * (1.0 + 1e-7)

        try:
            sh = np.asarray(shape, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if sh.size != L:
                sh = np.resize(sh, L)
            sh = np.where(np.isfinite(sh), sh, 0.0)
            sh = sh - np.min(sh)
            if sh.max() <= 1e-300:
                return np.full(L, avg)
            sh_sum = float(sh.sum())
            if sh_sum <= 1e-300:
                return np.full(L, avg)
            q = sh / sh_sum
        except Exception:
            return np.full(L, avg)

        def sum_at(lam):
            return float(np.sum(np.minimum(mcap, lam * q)))

        hi = max(1.0, mcap / max(float(q.max()), 1e-300))
        s_at_hi = sum_at(hi)
        for _ in range(100):
            if s_at_hi >= 1.0:
                break
            hi *= 2.0
            s_at_hi = sum_at(hi)
            if hi > 1e15:
                break

        lo = 0.0
        if s_at_hi < 1.0:
            return np.full(L, avg)
        for _ in range(80):
            mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
            if sum_at(mid) < 1.0:
                lo = mid
            else:
                hi = mid

        b = np.minimum(mcap, hi * q)

        for _ in range(10):
            s = float(b.sum())
            if not finite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
                return np.full(L, avg)
            if abs(s - 1.0) <= 1e-12:
                break
            if s < 1.0:
                rem = 1.0 - s
                room = np.maximum(mcap - b, 0.0)
                tr = float(room.sum())
                if tr < rem - 1e-12:
                    return np.full(L, avg)
                b = b + rem * (room / tr)
                b = np.minimum(b, mcap)
            else:
                b = b / s
                b = np.minimum(b, mcap)

        s = float(b.sum())
        if s > 1e-300:
            b = b / s
        return b

    def root_from_B(b, n):
        try:
            if b.size != 2 * n - 1:
                return None
            b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(b)) or b[0] <= 0.0:
                return None
            a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            a0 = math.sqrt(float(b[0]))
            if a0 < 1e-12:
                return None
            a[0] = a0
            for k in range(1, n):
                s = float(b[k])
                if k > 1:
                    s -= float(np.dot(a[1:k], a[k - 1:0:-1]))
                val = s / (2.0 * a0)
                if val < -1e-7:
                    return None
                if val < 0.0:
                    val = 0.0
                a[k] = val
            a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
            s = float(a.sum())
            if s <= 1e-12:
                return None
            return a / s
        except Exception:
            return None

    def root_candidates(n, max_count=35, stop_gen=None):
        out = []
        try:
            L = 2 * n - 1
            r = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, L)
            d = 2.0 * r - 1.0
            shapes = [
                np.ones(L),
                1.0 + 1.0 * np.cos(np.pi * r),
                1.0 + 0.7 * np.cos(np.pi * r),
                1.0 - 0.7 * np.cos(np.pi * r),
                1.0 + 0.7 * d * d,
                1.0 - 0.5 * d * d,
                np.exp(-d * d / 0.10),
                np.exp(-d * d / 0.40),
                np.exp(-2.0 * r * r) + np.exp(-2.0 * (1.0 - r) * (1.0 - r)),
                np.exp(-2.0 * (r - 0.25) * (r - 0.25)) + np.exp(-2.0 * (r - 0.75) * (r - 0.75)),
                1.0 / (1.0 + 3.0 * d * d),
                1.0 + 3.0 * r * (1.0 - r),
                1.0 + 3.0 * (r * (1.0 - r)) * (r * (1.0 - r)),
                1.0 + 5.0 * (1.0 - r * (1.0 - r)),
            ]
            target_m = TARGET / (2.0 * n)
            caps = [1.00, 1.02, 1.05, 1.10, 1.20, 1.40, 1.80]
            for sh in shapes:
                for cf in caps:
                    if stop_gen is not None and time.monotonic() >= stop_gen:
                        return out
                    b = cap_profile(n, sh, target_m * cf)
                    if b is None:
                        continue
                    a = root_from_B(b, n)
                    if a is None:
                        continue
                    a = to_sum1(a)
                    if a is None:
                        continue
                    out.append(a.copy())
                    arev = a[::-1].copy()
                    asym = 0.5 * (a + a[::-1])
                    asum = float(asym.sum())
                    if asum > 1e-300:
                        asym = asy/ asy.sum() if False else asym / asum
                    out.append(arev)
                    out.append(to_sum1(asym) if to_sum1(asym) is not None else arev)
                    if len(out) >= max_count:
                        return out
        except Exception:
            pass
        return out

    def gen_candidates(n, stop_gen):
        out = []
        if n <= 0:
            return out
        try:
            u = np.ones(n) / n
            out.append(u.copy())

            out.extend(root_candidates(n, max_count=30, stop_gen=stop_gen))
            if time.monotonic() >= stop_gen:
                return out

            r = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
            alphas = [0.2, 0.4, 0.6, 0.8, 0.95]
            for alpha in alphas:
                a = np.clip(1.0 + alpha * np.cos(np.pi * r), 1e-12, None)
                s = float(a.sum())
                if s > 1e-300:
                    out.append(a / s)

            for q in (2, 4, 8):
                for alpha in (0.4, 0.9, 1.8, 3.2):
                    a = np.clip(1.0 + alpha * np.power(2.0 * r - 1.0, q), 1e-12, None)
                    s = float(a.sum())
                    if s > 1e-300:
                        out.append(a / s)

            for ratio in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0, 10.0):
                a = u.copy()
                a[0] = u[0] * ratio
                a[-1] = u[-1] * ratio
                s = float(a.sum())
                if s > 1e-300:
                    out.append(a / s)

            for sigma in (0.15, 0.35, 0.70):
                for _ in range(2):
                    a = np.abs(rng.normal(1.0, sigma, n)) + 1e-8
                    a = 0.5 * (a + a[::-1])
                    s = float(a.sum())
                    if s > 1e-300:
                        out.append(a / s)
                    a = np.exp(sigma * rng.standard_normal(n)) + 1e-8
                    a = 0.5 * (a + a[::-1])
                    s = float(a.sum())
                    if s > 1e-300:
                        out.append(a / s)

            if time.monotonic() >= stop_gen:
                return out

            for num_blocks in (2, 3, 4):
                block = max(1, n // num_blocks)
                a = u.copy()
                for b in range(num_blocks):
                    loc = rng.integers(0, max(1, n - block))
                    amp = 0.1 + 1.0 * rng.random()
                    a[loc:loc + block] *= amp
                    s = float(a.sum())
                    if s > 1e-300:
                        out.append(a / s)

        except Exception:
            pass
        return out

    try:
        if parent is not None:
            base_len = int(parent.size)
        else:
            base_len = 320
        lengths = []
        candidates_l = [
            base_len,
            max(32, base_len - 32),
            base_len + 32,
            base_len + 64,
            256,
            320,
            384,
            512,
        ]
        for L in candidates_l:
            if 32 <= int(L) <= 768:
                lengths.append(int(L))
        lengths = sorted(set(lengths))
        if len(lengths) > 6:
            lengths = sorted(lengths, key=lambda x: (abs(x - base_len), x))[:6]
    except Exception:
        lengths = [320]

    pool = []
    gen_stop = min(deadline - 220.0, t0 + 120.0)
    for L in lengths:
        if time.monotonic() >= gen_stop:
            break
        stop_here = max(time.monotonic() + 5.0, min(gen_stop - 5.0, time.monotonic() + 25.0))
        cands = []
        if parent is not None:
            cands.append(interpolate(parent, L))
        cands.extend(gen_candidates(L, stop_here))
        for c in cands:
            a = to_sum1(c)
            if a is None:
                continue
            f = fast_score(a)
            if finite(f):
                pool.append((f, a))

    pool.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])

    def similar(a, b):
        try:
            if a.size != b.size:
                return False
            return float(np.max(np.abs(a - b))) < 1e-6
        except Exception:
            return False

    selected = []
    for f, a in pool:
        if len(selected) >= 8:
            break
        if any(similar(a, b) for b in selected):
            continue
        selected.append(a.copy())

    # Ensure uniform fallback is in the pool; parent may have been recorded already.
    if parent is not None and not any(similar(parent, x) for x in selected):
        selected.insert(0, parent.copy())
    fallback_selected = np.ones(max(32, base_len)) / float(max(32, base_len))
    if not any(similar(fallback_selected, x) for x in selected):
        selected.append(fallback_selected.copy())

    for a in selected[:8]:
        record_candidate(a)

    def coordinate_descent(a, max_passes=10, stop=None, random_order=False):
        try:
            arr = to_sum1(a)
            if arr is None:
                return a
            n = int(arr.size)
            if n < 2:
                return arr
            if stop is None:
                stop = time.monotonic() + 60.0

            b = np.convolve(arr, arr)
            M = float(b.max())
            best_a = arr.copy()
            best_M = M
            update_fast(best_a, 2.0 * n * best_M)

            passes = 0
            while time.monotonic() < stop and passes < max_passes:
                order = rng.permutation(n) if random_order else np.arange(n)
                improved = 0
                for i in order:
                    if time.monotonic() >= stop - 0.2:
                        break
                    y = float(arr[i])
                    y = max(0.0, y)
                    c0 = 1.0 - y
                    if c0 <= 1e-15 and y > 0.99999:
                        y = 0.99999
                        c0 = 1.0 - y

                    # affected convolution indices: k = i + j, j=0..n-1
                    base = np.asarray(arr[b: b + n] if False else np.convolve(arr, arr)[i:i + n], dtype=np.float64)
                    # We already have b; do not reconvolve here.
                    base = b[i:i + n].copy()
                    js = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
                    a_js = arr[js]

                    is_quad = (js == i)
                    old = 2.0 * y * a_js
                    if is_quad.any():
                        old[is_quad] = y * y
                    base = base - old

                    left = b[:i] if i > 0 else np.array([0.0])
                    right = b[i + n:] if i + n < b.size else np.array([0.0])
                    C0 = float(max(float(np.max(left)), float(np.max(right))))

                    mask = ~is_quad
                    if mask.any():
                        lb = base[mask].copy()
                        lc = (2.0 * a_js[mask]).copy()
                    else:
                        lb = np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
                        lc = np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
                    qb = float(base[i]) if is_quad.any() else None

                    x_hi = min(
                        50.0,
                        max(
                            5.0,
                            10.0 * max(y, 1e-8),
                            10.0 * max(1.0 - y, 1e-8),
                        ),
                    )

                    def F(x):
                        x = float(x)
                        if x < 0.0:
                            x = 0.0
                        den = c0 + x
                        if den <= 1e-15:
                            return 1e300
                        m = C0
                        if lb.size:
                            mm = float(np.max(lb + lc * x))
                            if mm > m:
                                m = mm
                        if qb is not None:
                            qq = qb + x * x
                            if qq > m:
                                m = qq
                        return m / (den * den)

                    xs = [0.0, min(y, x_hi), x_hi]
                    if y > 0.0:
                        for rr in (0.01, 0.05, 0.15, 0.35, 0.6, 1.0, 1.4, 1.9, 2.8, 4.5, 8.0):
                            xx = y * rr
                            if xx <= x_hi:
                                xs.append(xx)
                    for rv in (0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.35, 0.55, 0.75, 0.9):
                        if rv < 1.0:
                            xx = rv * max(1.0 - y, 1e-9) / (1.0 - rv)
                            if 0.0 <= xx <= x_hi:
                                xs.append(xx)
                    if x_hi > 0.0:
                        xs.extend(np.linspace(0.0, x_hi, 12).tolist())
                    xs = np.array(xs, dtype=np.float64)
                    xs = np.unique(np.clip(xs, 0.0, x_hi))

                    best_x = float(y)
                    best_f = F(best_x)
                    cur_f = F(best_x)
                    if cur_f < best_f:
                        best_f = cur_f

                    try:
                        fs = np.array([F(float(x)) for x in xs], dtype=np.float64)
                        fs = np.where(np.isfinite(fs), fs, 1e300)
                        idx = int(np.argmin(fs))
                        if fs[idx] < best_f:
                            best_f = float(fs[idx])
                            best_x = float(xs[idx])
                    except Exception:
                        pass

                    if minimize_scalar is not None and best_x > 0.0 and best_x < x_hi:
                        lo = max(0.0, best_x * 0.35)
                        hi = min(x_hi, best_x * 2.9)
                        if hi > lo:
                            try:
                                res = minimize_scalar(
                                    F,
                                    bounds=(lo, hi),
                                    method="bounded",
                                    options={"xatol": 1e-8, "maxiter": 50},
                                )
                                if finite(res.fun) and res.fun < best_f:
                                    best_f = float(res.fun)
                                    best_x = float(res.x)
                            except Exception:
                                pass

                    if minimize_scalar is not None:
                        try:
                            res = minimize_scalar(
                                F,
                                bounds=(0.0, x_hi),
                                method="bounded",
                                options={"xatol": 1e-8, "maxiter": 60},
                            )
                            if finite(res.fun) and res.fun < best_f:
                                best_f = float(res.fun)
                                best_x = float(res.x)
                        except Exception:
                            pass

                    if best_f < M * (1.0 - 1e-11) and (c0 + max(0.0, best_x)) > 1e-12:
                        arr[i] = max(0.0, float(best_x))
                        np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0, out=arr)
                        s = float(arr.sum())
                        if s <= 1e-300:
                            arr = to_sum1(best_a)
                            break
                        arr = arr / s
                        b = np.convolve(arr, arr)
                        M = float(b.max())
                        if M < best_M:
                            best_M = M
                            best_a = arr.copy()
                            update_fast(best_a, 2.0 * n * best_M)
                        improved += 1

                if improved == 0:
                    break
                passes += 1

            if best_M < M:
                arr = best_a
            return arr
        except Exception:
            return a

    def sym_improve(a):
        try:
            arr = to_sum1(a)
            if arr is None:
                return a
            sym = 0.5 * (arr + arr[::-1])
            s = float(sym.sum())
            if s <= 1e-300:
                return arr
            sym = sym / s
            if fast_score(sym) <= fast_score(arr):
                return sym
            return arr
        except Exception:
            return a

    def lp_improve(a, stop, max_solves=8):
        try:
            arr = to_sum1(a)
            if arr is None:
                return a
            n = int(arr.size)
            if n < 10 or linprog is None or sps is None:
                return a
            if stop is None:
                stop = time.monotonic() + 90.0

            current = arr.copy()
            curM = fast_score(current)
            best = current.copy()
            bestM = curM
            update_fast(best, bestM)

            for _ in range(max_solves):
                if time.monotonic() >= stop:
                    break
                b = np.convolve(current, current)
                M = float(b.max())
                if not finite(M) or M <= 0.0:
                    break
                rows, cols, idxp = _conv_pattern(n)
                vals = current[idxp]
                try:
                    A = sps.csr_matrix((vals, (rows, cols)), shape=(2 * n - 1, n))
                except Exception:
                    break
                c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
                improved = False

                for fac in (1.000, 1.002, 1.005, 1.010, 1.020, 1.040):
                    if time.monotonic() >= stop:
                        break
                    bub = np.full(2 * n - 1, M * fac, dtype=np.float64)
                    res = None
                    for opts in ({"presolve": True}, {"presolve": True, "time_limit": 5.0}):
                        try:
                            res = linprog(
                                c,
                                A_ub=A,
                                b_ub=bub,
                                bounds=[(0.0, None)] * n,
                                method="highs",
                                options=opts,
                            )
                            break
                        except Exception:
                            res = None

                    if res is None:
                        continue
                    ok = bool(getattr(res, "success", False)) or int(getattr(res, "status", 1)) == 0
                    if not ok:
                        continue
                    gx = np.asarray(getattr(res, "x", None), dtype=np.float64)
                    if gx is None or gx.size != n:
                        continue
                    gx = np.clip(gx, 0.0, None)
                    sg = float(gx.sum())
                    if sg <= 1e-12:
                        continue
                    g = gx / sg

                    accepted = False
                    for t in (0.08, 0.15, 0.25, 0.40, 0.60, 0.80, 1.00):
                        cand = (1.0 - t) * current + t * g
                        cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, None)
                        s = float(cand.sum())
                        if s <= 1e-12:
                            continue
                        cand = cand / s
                        f = fast_score(cand)
                        if finite(f) and f < bestM * (1.0 - 1e-12):
                            current = cand
                            bestM = f
                            best = cand.copy()
                            update_fast(best, bestM)
                            accepted = True
                            break
                    if accepted:
                        improved = True
                        break

                if not improved:
                    break

                if time.monotonic() < stop - 15.0:
                    current = coordinate_descent(current, max_passes=2, stop=min(stop - 5.0, time.monotonic() + 35.0))
                    current = sym_improve(current)
                    f = fast_score(current)
                    if finite(f) and f < bestM:
                        bestM = f
                        best = current.copy()
                        update_fast(best, bestM)

            return best
        except Exception:
            return a

    def subgradient_optimize(a, stop, max_steps=80):
        try:
            arr = to_sum1(a)
            if arr is None:
                return a
            n = int(arr.size)
            if n < 8:
                return arr
            if stop is None:
                stop = time.monotonic() + 60.0

            current = arr.copy()
            curM = fast_score(current)
            best = current.copy()
            bestM = curM
            update_fast(best, bestM)

            stale = 0
            for step in range(max_steps):
                if time.monotonic() >= stop:
                    break
                b = np.convolve(current, current)
                M = float(b.max())
                if finite(M) and M < bestM:
                    bestM = M
                    best = current.copy()
                    update_fast(best, bestM)

                L = 2 * n - 1
                K = min(L, max(24, n // 3))
                order = np.argsort(b)[-K:]
                vals = b[order]
                tau = max(1e-12, 0.08 * (float(vals.max()) - float(vals.min()) + 1e-30))
                w = np.exp((vals - vals.max()) / tau)
                sw = float(w.sum())
                if sw <= 1e-300:
                    w = np.ones_like(vals)
                    sw = w.sum()
                else:
                    w = w / sw

                d = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                for kk, ww in zip(order, w):
                    kk = int(kk)
                    lo = max(0, kk - n + 1)
                    hi = min(n - 1, kk)
                    idx = np.arange(lo, hi + 1, dtype=np.int64)
                    d[idx] += 2.0 * float(ww) * current[kk - idx]

                d = d - float(d.mean())
                dm = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
                if dm <= 1e-15:
                    break
                d = d / dm

                accepted = False
                for eta in (1e-5, 3e-5, 1e-4, 3e-4, 1e-3, 3e-3, 1e-2, 3e-2, 1e-1):
                    cand = current - eta * d
                    cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, None)
                    s = float(cand.sum())
                    if s <= 1e-12:
                        continue
                    cand = cand / s
                    f = fast_score(cand)
                    if finite(f) and f < M * (1.0 - 1e-12):
                        current = cand
                        M = f
                        accepted = True
                        stale = 0
                        break

                if not accepted:
                    stale += 1
                    cand = current * np.exp(0.04 * rng.standard_normal(n))
                    cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, None)
                    s = float(cand.sum())
                    if s > 1e-12:
                        cand = cand / s
                        f = fast_score(cand)
                        if finite(f) and f < bestM:
                            current = cand
                            bestM = f
                            best = cand.copy()
                            update_fast(best, bestM)
                    if stale > 18:
                        break

                if finite(M) and M < bestM:
                    bestM = M
                    best = current.copy()
                    update_fast(best, bestM)

            return best
        except Exception:
            return a

    def random_improve(a, stop, max_iters=200):
        try:
            arr = to_sum1(a)
            if arr is None:
                return a
            n = int(arr.size)
            if n < 8:
                return arr
            if stop is None:
                stop = time.monotonic() + 40.0

            current = arr.copy()
            curM = fast_score(current)
            best = current.copy()
            bestM = curM
            update_fast(best, bestM)
            stale = 0

            for _ in range(max_iters):
                if time.monotonic() >= stop:
                    break
                r = rng.random()
                if r < 0.45:
                    sigma = 0.05 + 0.25 * max(0.0, 1.0 - (time.monotonic() - t0) / max(1.0, stop - t0))
                    cand = current * np.exp(sigma * rng.standard_normal(n))
                elif r < 0.75:
                    cand = current.copy()
                    i, j = tuple(int(x) for x in rng.integers(0, n, size=2))
                    if i == j:
                        continue
                    t = float(rng.choice((0.1, 0.3, 0.5, 0.7, 1.0)))
                    old_i = cand[i]
                    old_j = cand[j]
                    cand[i] = (1.0 - t) * old_i + t * old_j
                    cand[j] = t * old_i + (1.0 - t) * old_j
                else:
                    cand = current.copy()
                    mask = rng.random(n) < 0.08
                    if mask.sum() == 0:
                        continue
                    fac = np.ones(int(mask.sum()), dtype=np.float64)
                    fac *= rng.choice((0.25, 0.5, 2.0, 4.0), size=int(mask.sum()))
                    cand[mask] *= fac

                cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, None)
                s = float(cand.sum())
                if s <= 1e-12:
                    continue
                cand = cand / s
                f = fast_score(cand)
                if finite(f) and f < bestM:
                    current = cand
                    bestM = f
                    best = cand.copy()
                    update_fast(best, bestM)
                    stale = 0
                else:
                    stale += 1
                    if stale > 25:
                        current = best.copy()
                        stale = 0
                    if stale > 80:
                        break
            return best
        except Exception:
            return a

    def deep_optimize(a, stop):
        try:
            arr = to_sum1(a)
            if arr is None:
                return a
            if stop is None:
                stop = time.monotonic() + 120.0

            current = arr.copy()
            curM = fast_score(current)
            best = current.copy()
            bestM = curM
            update_fast(best, bestM)

            # First solid coordinate polish.
            cstop = min(stop - 60.0, time.monotonic() + 180.0)
            current = coordinate_descent(current, max_passes=8, stop=cstop)
            current = sym_improve(current)
            f = fast_score(current)
            if finite(f) and f <= bestM:
                current = current
                best = current.copy()
                bestM = f
                update_fast(best, bestM)

            phase = 0
            while time.monotonic() < stop - 25.0:
                if phase == 0:
                    s2 = min(time.monotonic() + 80.0, stop - 25.0)
                    cand = coordinate_descent(current, max_passes=4, stop=s2)
                    cand = sym_improve(cand)
                    f = fast_score(cand)
                    if finite(f) and f < bestM:
                        current = cand
                        best = cand.copy()
                        bestM = f
                        update_fast(best, bestM)
                elif phase == 1:
                    s2 = min(time.monotonic() + 120.0, stop - 25.0)
                    cand = lp_improve(current, stop=s2, max_solves=6)
                    f = fast_score(cand)
                    if finite(f) and f < bestM:
                        current = cand
                        best = cand.copy()
                        bestM = f
                        update_fast(best, bestM)
                elif phase == 2:
                    s2 = min(time.monotonic() + 60.0, stop - 25.0)
                    cand = subgradient_optimize(current, stop=s2, max_steps=70)
                    f = fast_score(cand)
                    if finite(f) and f < bestM:
                        current = cand
                        best = cand.copy()
                        bestM = f
                        update_fast(best, bestM)
                else:
                    s2 = min(time.monotonic() + 40.0, stop - 25.0)
                    cand = random_improve(current, stop=s2, max_iters=120)
                    f = fast_score(cand)
                    if finite(f) and f < bestM:
                        current = cand
                        best = cand.copy()
                        bestM = f
                        update_fast(best, bestM)
                    sym = sym_improve(current)
                    fs = fast_score(sym)
                    if finite(fs) and fs < bestM:
                        current = sym
                        best = sym.copy()
                        bestM = fs
                        update_fast(best, bestM)
                phase = (phase + 1) % 4

            current = coordinate_descent(current, max_passes=3, stop=max(time.monotonic() + 1.0, stop - 5.0))
            current = sym_improve(current)
            f = fast_score(current)
            if finite(f) and f < bestM:
                best = current.copy()
                bestM = f
                update_fast(best, bestM)

            return best
        except Exception:
            return a

    # Quick multi-start coordinate polish.
    phaseA_stop = min(deadline - 180.0, t0 + 240.0)
    for i, a in enumerate(selected[:8]):
        if time.monotonic() >= phaseA_stop:
            break
        alloc = min(50.0, phaseA_stop - time.monotonic() - 5.0)
        if alloc < 8.0:
            break
        a2 = coordinate_descent(a, max_passes=4, stop=time.monotonic() + alloc, random_order=(i % 2 == 1))
        a2 = sym_improve(a2)
        record_candidate(a2)
        selected[i] = a2

    selected.sort(key=lambda x: fast_score(x))
    deep_list = []
    for a in selected[:10]:
        if len(deep_list) >= 3:
            break
        if any(similar(a, b) for b in deep_list):
            continue
        deep_list.append(a.copy())

    for i, a in enumerate(deep_list):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 90.0:
            break
        alloc = min(650.0 if i == 0 else 300.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 70.0)
        if alloc < 35.0:
            break
        a2 = deep_optimize(a, stop=time.monotonic() + alloc)
        a2 = sym_improve(a2)
        record_candidate(a2)

    if best_fast_arr is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
        a = best_fast_arr.copy()
        a = coordinate_descent(a, max_passes=3, stop=min(deadline - 8.0, time.monotonic() + 160.0))
        a = sym_improve(a)
        record_candidate(a)

    if best_fast_arr is not None:
        record_candidate(best_fast_arr)

    if best_arr is None:
        fb = parent if parent is not None else np.ones(320) / 320.0
        record_exact(fb)

    if best_arr is None:
        fb = np.ones(320) / 320.0
        fb = to_sum1(fb)
        if fb is None:
            fb = np.ones(320, dtype=np.float64)
        # last-resort manual valid return
        arr = np.clip(np.where(np.isfinite(fb), fb, 0.0), 0.0, 1000.0)
        if arr.size == 0 or float(arr.sum()) < 0.01:
            arr = np.ones(320, dtype=np.float64)
        return [float(x) for x in arr]

    final = np.asarray(best_arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    final = np.where(np.isfinite(final), final, 0.0)
    final = np.clip(final, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(final.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        final = np.ones(320, dtype=np.float64)
    return [float(x) for x in final]
```
